Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any petrochemical module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 851. The projective resolution has length at most 394 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 851 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the petrochemical structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 394. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)
